    48	// retention (last 50 entries / 7 days). The log rides .handoff/ so the purge
    49	// walk covers it.
    50	const HOOK_LOG_MAX = 50;
    51	const HOOK_LOG_MAX_AGE_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    52	
    53	/** @param {any} io @param {string} event @param {any} err */
    54	function logHookError(io, event, err) {
    55	  try {
    56	    // The diagnostics log rides .handoff/, so it passes the same managed-tree
    57	    // jail as every other write (threat model): refuse to create or write
    58	    // through a symlinked/escaped .handoff. Diagnostics must never be the hole
    59	    // the jail closes elsewhere — and this stays fail-open (skip, don't throw).
    60	    if (!checkHandoffTree(io.cwd, io).ok) return;
    61	    const dir = `${io.cwd}/.handoff/log`;
    62	    io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    63	    const path = `${dir}/hook-errors.jsonl`;
    64	    const nowIso = typeof io.now === 'function' ? io.now() : new Date().toISOString();
    65	    /** @type {any[]} */
    66	    let entries = [];
    67	    try {
    68	      entries = io.fs
    69	        .readFileSync(path, 'utf8')
    70	        .split('\n')
    71	        .filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l.trim() !== '')

    73	          try {
    74	            return JSON.parse(l);
    75	          } catch {
    76	            return null;
    77	          }
    78	        })
    79	        .filter(Boolean);
    80	    } catch {
    81	      entries = [];
    82	    }
    83	    entries.push({ ts: nowIso, event, code: err?.code ?? null });
    84	    const cutoff = Date.parse(nowIso) - HOOK_LOG_MAX_AGE_MS;
    85	    entries = entries.filter((e) => typeof e.ts === 'string' && Date.parse(e.ts) >= cutoff).slice(-HOOK_LOG_MAX);
    86	    io.fs.writeFileSync(path, entries.map((e) => JSON.stringify(e)).join('\n') + '\n');
    87	  } catch {
    88	    // diagnostics must never break fail-open
    89	  }
    90	}
    91	
    92	/**
    93	 * Run a core baton command as a child process, forwarding the raw hook payload
    94	 * as the child's stdin. Rejections are swallowed (fail-open) — core commands
    95	 * carry their own hook-safety, and Claude ignores hook exit codes anyway —
    96	 * but each failure leaves a metadata-only diagnostic in .handoff/log/.
    97	 * opts.acceptCodes: exit codes that are VERDICTS, not failures (audit finding
    98	 * 20: detect's 10/11/12/13 classification exits were logged as hook errors on
    99	 * every real limit death, drowning the diagnostics log in noise).
   100	 * @param {any} io @param {string[]} argv @param {string} event
   101	 * @param {{timeout?: number, acceptCodes?: number[]}} [opts]
   102	 * @returns {Promise<{stdout: string, stderr: string} | null>}
   103	 */
   104	async function baton(io, argv, event, opts = {}) {
   105	  try {
   106	    return await io.execFile('node', [batonBin(io), ...argv], { input: io.stdin, timeout: opts.timeout });
   107	  } catch (err) {
   108	    if (Array.isArray(opts.acceptCodes) && opts.acceptCodes.includes(/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code)) {
   109	      const e = /** @type {any} */ (err);
   110	      return { stdout: e?.stdout ?? '', stderr: e?.stderr ?? '' };
   111	    }
   112	    // fail-open: a failing child must not break the session
   113	    logHookError(io, event, err);
   114	    return null;
   115	  }
   116	}
   117	
   118	/** @param {any} io @returns {any | null} parsed hook payload, or null */
   119	function parsePayload(io) {
   120	  try {
   121	    return JSON.parse(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   122	  } catch {
   123	    return null;
   124	  }
   125	}

   128	 * SessionStart: not a checkpoint — delegate to the core `session-start`

   130	 * `${io.cwd}/.handoff/bundle.json` raw, so launching Claude Code in a repo
   131	 * SUBDIRECTORY silently never fired the pending-handoff notice while the same
   132	 * session's Stop checkpoints DID land at the discovered toplevel). Core owns
   133	 * root discovery, the managed-tree jail, and the origin allowlist; this shim
   134	 * only forwards the shaped stdout the harness consumes.
   135	 * @param {any} io
   136	 */
   137	async function sessionStart(io) {
   138	  const r = await baton(io, ['session-start', '--platform', 'claude-code'], 'SessionStart', { timeout: 8_000 });
   139	  if (r && typeof r.stdout === 'string' && r.stdout.length > 0) io.stdout.write(r.stdout);
   140	  return 0;
   141	}
   142	
   143	/**
   144	 * @param {string[]} args argv after the script path; args[0] is the hook event
   145	 * @param {any} io injected {cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now, …}
   146	 * @returns {Promise<number>} always 0 — fail-open by construction
   147	 */
   148	export async function runHook(args, io) {
   149	  try {
   150	    // Supervised loop children never touch or read any bundle: fast-path out
   151	    // before spawning anything (mirrors core isSupervisedChild — this script
   152	    // runs standalone, so the check is inlined).
   153	    const supervised = io?.env?.BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD;
   154	    if (typeof supervised === 'string' && supervised.length > 0) return 0;
   155	
   156	    const event = args[0] ?? 'unknown';
   157	
   158	    if (event === 'SessionStart') return sessionStart(io);
   159	
   160	    if (event === 'StopFailure') {
   161	      // CHECKPOINT FIRST (audit finding 22): it is the part that must land
   162	      // before the harness's 30s envelope kills the hook — the limit-death
   163	      // checkpoint is the whole point of this event. Explicit per-child
   164	      // timeouts sum under the envelope (18s + 8s < 30s). detect runs second,
   165	      // and its classification exit codes are VERDICTS, not errors (finding
   166	      // 20) — only real failures (1/2/spawn) reach the diagnostics log.
   167	      await baton(io, ['checkpoint', '--platform', 'claude-code', '--trigger', 'StopFailure'], event, { timeout: 18_000 });
   168	      const errorType = parsePayload(io)?.error?.type;
   169	      const detectArgs = ['detect', '--platform', 'claude-code'];
   170	      if (typeof errorType === 'string') detectArgs.push('--structured-error-type', errorType);
   171	      await baton(io, detectArgs, event, { timeout: 8_000, acceptCodes: [0, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14] });
   172	      return 0;
   173	    }
   174	
   175	    // Stop / PreCompact / SessionEnd — and any future event — are routine
   176	    // mechanical checkpoints; core normalize degrades unknown payloads safely.
   177	    await baton(io, ['checkpoint', '--platform', 'claude-code'], event);
   178	    return 0;
   179	  } catch {
   180	    return 0;
   181	  }
   182	}
   183	
   184	// Main entry: hooks.json executes this file directly (`node …/hook.mjs <Event>`),
   185	// so a real invocation must build the real io and run — an export alone is a
   186	// production no-op (gate-2 reviewer-b finding 1).
   187	const isMain = (() => {
   188	  try {
   189	    return process.argv[1] ? import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href : false;
   190	  } catch {
   191	    return false;
   192	  }
   193	})();
   194	
   195	if (isMain) {
   196	  let stdin = '';
   197	  try {
   198	    if (!process.stdin.isTTY) stdin = readFileSync(0, 'utf8');
   199	  } catch {
   200	    stdin = '';
   201	  }
   202	  const io = {
   203	    cwd: process.cwd(),
   204	    env: process.env,
   205	    stdin,
   206	    stdout: process.stdout,
   207	    stderr: process.stderr,
   208	    fs: nodeFs,
   209	    execFile: execFileWithInput,
   210	    now: () => new Date().toISOString(),
   211	  };
   212	  runHook(process.argv.slice(2), io).then(
   213	    (code) => process.exit(code),
   214	    () => process.exit(0),
   215	  );
   216	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], avoidEntries?: Array<{platform: string, model: string}>, nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], avoidEntries = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  // Entry-level avoidance (plan §Model-level failover): a specific
    54	  // {platform, model} tuple is dead (e.g. account-tier rejected) while its
    55	  // platform stays usable. Matching is on model IDENTITY — an @effort suffix
    56	  // on either side is ignored — and requires BOTH dimensions, so the same
    57	  // model name on another platform is never caught.
    58	  /** @param {any} m */
    59	  const baseModel = (m) => (typeof m === 'string' ? m.split('@')[0] : m);
    60	  /** @param {string} platform @param {string} model */
    61	  const entryAvoided = (platform, model) =>
    62	    avoidEntries.some((a) => a?.platform === platform && baseModel(a?.model) === baseModel(model));
    63	
    64	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    65	  const assignments = {};
    66	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    67	    /** @type {any[]} */
    68	    const skipped = [];
    69	    /** @type {any} */
    70	    let assignment = null;
    71	
    72	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    73	      const entry = chain[i];
    74	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    75	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    76	        continue;
    77	      }
    78	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    79	      if (why) {
    80	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    81	        continue;
    82	      }
    83	      // After skipReason so a dual match keeps the platform-level label.
    84	      if (entryAvoided(entry.platform, entry.model)) {
    85	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'avoided-entry' });
    86	        continue;
    87	      }
    88	      assignment = {
    89	        platform: entry.platform,
    90	        model: entry.model,
    91	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    92	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    93	        chainIndex: i,
    94	        skipped,
    95	      };
    96	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    97	      break;
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
   101	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
   102	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
   103	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
   104	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
   105	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   106	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
   107	      }
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    if (!assignment) {
   111	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   112	    }
   113	    assignments[role] = assignment;

     1	const PRECEDENCE = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'model-unavailable', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
     2	
     3	/** @type {Record<string, string>} */
     4	const STOP_FAILURE_MAP = {
     5	  rate_limit: 'usage-limit',
     6	  overloaded: 'throttle',
     7	  authentication_failed: 'auth',
     8	  oauth_org_not_allowed: 'auth',
     9	  billing_error: 'auth',
    10	};

    12	// eslint-disable-next-line no-control-regex
    13	const ANSI = /\x1b\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z]/g;
    14	
    15	/** @param {any} matcher @param {string} text */
    16	function matches(matcher, text) {
    17	  switch (matcher.kind) {
    18	    case 'substring':
    19	      return text.includes(matcher.value);
    20	    case 'regex':
    21	      return new RegExp(matcher.pattern, matcher.flags || '').test(text);
    22	    case 'json-field': {
    23	      for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    24	        let obj;
    25	        try {
    26	          obj = JSON.parse(line);
    27	        } catch {
    28	          continue;
    29	        }
    30	        const val = String(matcher.path)
    31	          .split('.')
    32	          .reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
    33	        if (val === matcher.equals) return true;
    34	      }
    35	      return false;
    36	    }
    37	    default:
    38	      return false;
    39	  }
    40	}
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Classify harness output. Structured evidence first (text and exit code are
    44	 * ignored when a recognized structured signal is present); text signatures as
    45	 * the fallback tier; exit code only breaks the no-match tie — a generic nonzero
    46	 * exit is never usage-limit on its own.
    47	 * @param {{text?: string, exitCode?: number, platform: string, table: any, structured?: {kind: string, errorType: string} | null}} input
    48	 * @returns {{class: string, signatureId: string | null, confidence: string | null, resetHint: string | null, tried?: string[]}}
    49	 */
    50	export function classify({ text = '', exitCode = 0, platform, table, structured = null }) {
    51	  if (structured) {
    52	    if (structured.kind === 'stop-failure') {
    53	      const cls = STOP_FAILURE_MAP[structured.errorType] ?? 'other-error';
    54	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    55	    }
    56	    if (structured.kind === 'exec-json') {
    57	      const cls = structured.errorType === 'usage_limit' ? 'usage-limit' : 'other-error';
    58	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    59	    }
    60	    // Unrecognized structured kind: fall through to the text tier.
    61	  }
    62	
    63	  const stripped = text.replace(ANSI, '');
    64	  const candidates = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform);
    65	  /** @type {any} */
    66	  let best = null;
    67	  for (const s of candidates) {
    68	    if (!matches(s.matcher, stripped)) continue;
    69	    if (best === null || PRECEDENCE.indexOf(s.class) < PRECEDENCE.indexOf(best.class)) best = s;
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  if (best) {
    73	    let resetHint = null;
    74	    if (typeof best.resetHint === 'string') {
    75	      const m = stripped.match(new RegExp(best.resetHint));
    76	      if (m) resetHint = m[1] ?? m[0];
    77	    }
    78	    return { class: best.class, signatureId: best.id, confidence: best.confidence, resetHint };
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  return {
    82	    class: exitCode !== 0 ? 'other-error' : 'ok',
    83	    signatureId: null,
    84	    confidence: null,
    85	    resetHint: null,
    86	  };
    87	}

    12	/**

    18	 * @returns {number}
    19	 */
    20	export function cmdDetect(args, io) {
    21	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', text: 'string', 'exit-code': 'string', 'structured-error-type': 'string', signatures: 'string', explain: 'boolean' });
    22	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'detect', parsed.error);
    23	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    24	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
    25	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'detect', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    26	
    27	  let table;
    28	  try {
    29	    table = loadSignatures(
    30	      {
    31	        builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES,
    32	        overlayPath: typeof flags.signatures === 'string' ? flags.signatures : undefined,
    33	      },
    34	      io,
    35	    );
    36	  } catch (err) {
    37	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    38	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-signatures', msg } });
    39	    else io.stderr.write(`baton detect: ${msg}\n`);
    40	    return 1;
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  const text = typeof flags.text === 'string' ? flags.text : typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '';
    44	  const exitCode = flags['exit-code'] !== undefined ? Number(flags['exit-code']) : 0;
    45	
    46	  /** @type {{kind: string, errorType: string} | null} */
    47	  let structured = null;
    48	  if (typeof flags['structured-error-type'] === 'string') {
    49	    structured = {
    50	      kind: platform === 'codex' ? 'exec-json' : 'stop-failure',
    51	      errorType: flags['structured-error-type'],
    52	    };
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  const verdict = classify({ text, exitCode, platform, table, structured });
    56	  const data = { ...verdict, tableVersion: table.schema, tableUpdated: table.updated };
    57	  if (flags.explain) {
    58	    data.tried = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform).map((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.id);
    59	  }
    60	
    61	  if (flags.json) {
    62	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
    63	  } else {
    64	    io.stdout.write(`${verdict.class}${verdict.signatureId ? ` (${verdict.signatureId}, ${verdict.confidence})` : ''}${verdict.resetHint ? ` — resets ${verdict.resetHint}` : ''}\n`);
    65	  }
    66	  return EXIT_BY_CLASS[verdict.class] ?? 13;
    67	}

    40	function git(cwd, args, env) {
    41	  const r = spawnSync('git', args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8', env: env ? { ...process.env, ...env } : process.env });
    42	  if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(' ')} failed: ${r.stderr || r.stdout}`);
    43	  return r.stdout;

    67	/** A real git repo, sole-author config, one initial commit, gitignore seeded. */
    68	function initRepo(prefix, spec) {
    69	  // Canonicalize: git resolves worktree paths to their realpath (on macOS the
    70	  // tmpdir is a /var -> /private/var symlink), so the repo root must be the
    71	  // realpath too or `git worktree list` paths won't match worktreePaths().
    72	  const cwd = nodeFs.realpathSync(mkTmp(prefix));
    73	  git(cwd, ['init', '-q', '-b', 'main']);
    74	  git(cwd, ['config', 'user.name', NAME]);
    75	  git(cwd, ['config', 'user.email', EMAIL]);
    76	  git(cwd, ['config', 'commit.gpgsign', 'false']);
    77	  // Pre-seed BOTH ignore lines so setupWorktrees is a no-op (constraint 5: the
    78	  // only tracked change would be the .worktrees/ line — pre-seeding removes it).
    79	  nodeFs.writeFileSync(join(cwd, '.gitignore'), '.handoff/\n.worktrees/\n');
    80	  nodeFs.writeFileSync(join(cwd, 'baton.config.json'), JSON.stringify(CONFIG, null, 2));
    81	  nodeFs.writeFileSync(join(cwd, 'README.md'), '# demo\n');
    82	  if (spec) nodeFs.writeFileSync(join(cwd, 'loop.json'), JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n');
    83	  git(cwd, ['add', '.']);
    84	  git(cwd, ['commit', '-q', '-m', 'initial']);
    85	  return cwd;
    86	}
    87	
    88	function makeRealIo(cwd, { superviseChild, now = NOW } = {}) {
    89	  const out = [];
    90	  const err = [];
    91	  let tokenN = 0;
    92	  return {
    93	    cwd,
    94	    env: { ...process.env },
    95	    stdin: '',
    96	    stdout: { write: (s) => (out.push(String(s)), true) },
    97	    stderr: { write: (s) => (err.push(String(s)), true) },
    98	    fs: nodeFs,
    99	    execFile: (cmd, args = [], opts = {}) => pexec(cmd, args, { ...opts, encoding: 'utf8' }),
   100	    now: () => now,
   101	    host: 'e2e-host',
   102	    pid: process.pid,
   103	    startTime: Math.round(performance.timeOrigin),
   104	    processAlive: (pid) => { try { process.kill(pid, 0); return true; } catch (e) { return /** @type {any} */ (e).code === 'EPERM'; } },
   105	    platform: process.platform,
   106	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${(tokenN += 1)}`,
   107	    superviseChild,
   108	    stdoutText: () => out.join(''),
   109	    stderrText: () => err.join(''),
   110	  };
   111	}

   113	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
   114	const mainLog = (cwd) => git(cwd, ['log', 'main', '--format=%an|%ae|%cn|%ce|%s']).trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean);
   115	const historyFreezes = (cwd) => {
   116	  const dir = bundlePaths(cwd).historyDir;

   119	};

   124	
   125	  // Writers (workspace-write) do REAL git commits in their seat worktree using
   126	  // the identity the supervisor injected via the child env (childSpec.env);
   127	  // reviewers + merger (read-only) just APPROVE.
   128	  function realWriterRunner() {
   129	    let n = 0;
   130	    return async (childSpec, opts) => {
   131	      const args = (childSpec.args ?? []).map(String);
   132	      if (valAfter(args, '-s') === 'workspace-write') {
   133	        const seat = valAfter(args, '-C');

   135	        nodeFs.writeFileSync(join(seat, `work-${n}.txt`), `change ${n}\n`);
   136	        git(seat, ['add', '.']);
   137	        git(seat, ['commit', '-q', '-m', `subtask change ${n}`], childSpec.env);
   138	      }
   139	      return { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath };
   140	    };
   141	  }

   143	  // Wrap the REAL execFile so we can prove the supervisor scanned the exact
   144	  // per-subtask ranges at merge time (finding 2 — no vacuous main..main).
   145	  async function runPipeline(prefix) {
   146	    const cwd = initRepo(prefix, pipeSpec);
   147	    const io = makeRealIo(cwd, { superviseChild: realWriterRunner() });
   148	    const gitLogRanges = [];

   150	    io.execFile = (cmd, args = [], opts = {}) => {
   151	      if (cmd === 'git' && args[0] === 'log') {
   152	        const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a)));
   153	        if (range) gitLogRanges.push(String(range));
   154	      }
   155	      return realExec(cmd, args, opts);
   156	    };
   157	    const { run } = await import('../../core/src/cli.mjs');
   158	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   159	    return { cwd, io, code, gitLogRanges };
   160	  }

   228	  // A scripted loop runner: returns the next result and writes its log so the
   229	  // supervisor classifies the transcript from the child's LOG file.
   230	  function scriptedRunner(script) {
   231	    const queue = [...script];
   232	    const calls = [];
   233	    const fn = async (childSpec, opts) => {
   234	      calls.push({ command: childSpec.command, args: (childSpec.args ?? []).map(String) });
   235	      const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   236	      if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   237	        nodeFs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
   238	        nodeFs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   239	      }
   240	      return { timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath };
   241	    };
   242	    fn.calls = calls;
   243	    return fn;
   244	  }

   292	describe('e2e — supervisor-death recovery via JOURNAL REPLAY on real fs (constraint 4)', () => {
   293	  it('a stale snapshot + a phase-advance journal event past its seq (with a torn tail) + a dead lock -> replay resumes, only the remaining phase spawns', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   294	    const spec = { schema: 'baton/loop@1', goal: 'e2e recovery', constraints: [], budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 }, phases: [{ id: 'p0', role: 'implementer' }, { id: 'p1', role: 'implementer' }] };
   295	    const cwd = initRepo('baton-e2e-recover-', spec);
   296	    const p = loopPaths(cwd);
   297	    nodeFs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   298	
   299	    // STALE snapshot: phase 0 not yet advanced, journalSeq behind the journal.
   300	    const staleSnapshot = {
   301	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1',
   302	      runId: 'loop-crashed',
   303	      goal: 'e2e recovery',
   304	      phaseCount: 2,
   305	      phaseIndex: 0, // the snapshot predates phase 0 completing
   306	      iterations: {},
   307	      status: 'running',
   308	      parkReason: null,
   309	      escalation: null,
   310	      smokeApproval: null,
   311	      createdAt: NOW,
   312	      journalSeq: 0,
   313	    };
   314	    nodeFs.writeFileSync(p.state, JSON.stringify(staleSnapshot, null, 2) + '\n');
   315	    // JOURNAL ahead of the snapshot: a committed phase-advance (seq 1, > snapshot's
   316	    // journalSeq 0) plus a TORN final line the tolerant reader must skip.
   317	    nodeFs.writeFileSync(
   318	      p.journal,
   319	      JSON.stringify({ seq: 1, ts: NOW, type: 'phase-advance' }) + '\n' + '{"seq":2,"type":"phase-adv', // torn tail, no newline
   320	    );
   321	    // A DEAD supervisor lock (a never-existing pid) — the crash marker.
   322	    nodeFs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'e2e-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'loop-crashed' }));
   323	
   324	    // Prove the fixture reconstructs mid-state via REPLAY, not a hand-written index:
   325	    // the snapshot says phaseIndex 0, the journal advances it to 1.
   326	    const io0 = makeRealIo(cwd);
   327	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState(cwd, io0)).state.phaseIndex, 1, 'journal replay advances the stale snapshot to phase 1');
   328	
   329	    // A fresh run reclaims the dead lock, replays to phase 1, and runs ONLY phase 1.
   330	    const runner = (() => { const calls = []; const fn = async (s, o) => { calls.push(s.command); return { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: o.logPath }; }; fn.calls = calls; return fn; })();
   331	    const io = makeRealIo(cwd, { superviseChild: runner });
   332	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   333	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the resumed run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   334	    assert.equal(runner.calls.length, 1, 'the journal-recovered phase 0 is NOT re-run — only phase 1 spawns a child');
   335	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState(cwd, io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE);
   336	  });
   337	});
   338	
   339	// ===========================================================================
   340	describe('e2e — loop leaves no residue (constraint 5, loop half)', () => {
   341	  it('after a completed loop run, removing .handoff leaves git status clean', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   342	    const spec = { schema: 'baton/loop@1', goal: 'e2e residue', constraints: [], budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 }, phases: [{ id: 'p0', role: 'implementer' }] };
   343	    const cwd = initRepo('baton-e2e-residue-', spec);
   344	    const io = makeRealIo(cwd, { superviseChild: async (s, o) => ({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: o.logPath }) });
   345	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   346	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the loop completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   347	
   348	    // The loop wrote only under the gitignored .handoff/ tree.
   349	    rmSync(join(cwd, '.handoff'), { recursive: true, force: true });
   350	    assert.equal(git(cwd, ['status', '--porcelain']).trim(), '', 'no tracked residue after a loop run (.gitignore pre-seeded)');
   351	  });
   352	});

   212	    // Phase 2: plan-reviewer -> codex/gpt-5.6-sol, reviewer read-only sandbox.
   213	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[1].command, 'codex');
   214	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '--model'), 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'the reviewer child carries the resolved model');
   215	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '-s'), 'read-only', 'the reviewer runs in a read-only sandbox');
   216	
   217	    // Every spawn is given a real per-child log path under .handoff/loop/children/
   218	    // plus its platform and timeout/grace budget.
   219	    for (const [i] of io.__runner.calls.entries()) {
   220	      const opts = io.__runner.calls[i].opts;
   221	      assert.match(String(opts.logPath), /\/\.handoff\/loop\/children\//, `child ${i} log path is under .handoff/loop/children/`);
   222	      assert.ok(typeof opts.platform === 'string' && opts.platform.length > 0, `child ${i} opts carry the platform`);
   223	      assert.ok(typeof opts.timeoutMs === 'number' && opts.timeoutMs > 0, `child ${i} opts carry a timeout`);
   224	      assert.ok(typeof opts.graceMs === 'number' && opts.graceMs > 0, `child ${i} opts carry a grace period`);
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   228	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached done');
   229	    assert.equal(state.phaseIndex, 2, 'both phases advanced');
   230	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'state.json'), 'loop state persisted under .handoff/loop');
   231	    assert.ok(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').journal), 'the loop journal persisted');
   232	  });
   233	});

   236	describe('loop run — gate iteration + the 5-cap', () => {

   239	  it('BLOCKED re-runs with findings in the retry prompt; the 6th attempt NEVER spawns; escalated + ESCALATION.md; exit 3', async () => {
   240	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: gateSpec, runner: undefined });

   254	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 5, 'exactly five gate attempts — a 6th is never spawned');

   263	    assert.ok(esc, 'an ESCALATION.md was written under .handoff/loop');

   268	// ===========================================================================
   269	describe('loop run — smoke gate', () => {
   270	  const smokeSpec = loopSpec({
   271	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
   272	    phases: [
   273	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
   274	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
   275	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
   276	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
   277	    ],
   278	  });
   279	
   280	  function smokeExecFile(rec) {
   281	    return (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   282	      rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
   283	      // git stays unavailable; the smoke command "passes".
   284	      if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   285	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
   286	    };
   287	  }
   288	
   289	  it('after smoke-build: runs smoke.cmd, writes SMOKE-REVIEW.md + a smoke-approval token, awaits, exit 0, no further child', async () => {
   290	    const rec = [];
   291	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   292	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   293	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   294	
   295	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   296	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'pausing for smoke approval is a clean exit 0');
   297	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 3, 'the smoke slice ran (3 phases); the post-smoke phase did NOT spawn');
   298	    assert.ok(rec.some((c) => /smoke/.test(c)), 'smoke.cmd was executed via io.execFile');
   299	
   300	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md'), 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
   301	    const approval = loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json');
   302	    assert.ok(approval, 'a smoke-approval record was written');
   303	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
   304	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'the approval carries a smokeApprovalToken over the six inputs');
   305	
   306	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   307	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run is awaiting smoke approval');
   308	  });
   309	
   310	  it('--approve-smoke <correct token> resumes into the next phase (a child spawns again)', async () => {
   311	    const rec = [];
   312	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   313	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   314	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   315	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   316	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token;
   317	
   318	    // Resume with the correct token — a FRESH runner for the continued phase.
   319	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   320	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   321	    io.__runner = runner2;
   322	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
   323	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   324	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 1, 'a verified approval continues into the post-smoke phase');
   325	  });
   326	
   327	  it('--approve-smoke with the REAL issued token after a bound input drifted -> refused: exit 4, PARKED, zero spawns', async () => {
   328	    // A git-aware execFile whose HEAD is a mutable ref — HEAD is a bound token
   329	    // input, so moving it between issuance and approval is genuine drift.
   330	    let head = 'HEAD-A';
   331	    const gitAware = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   332	      if (cmd === 'git') {
   333	        const key = args.join(' ');
   334	        const out = key === 'rev-parse HEAD' ? head : key === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' ? 'main' : '';
   335	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: out + '\n', stderr: '' });
   336	      }
   337	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' }); // the smoke command
   338	    };
   339	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: gitAware, runner: undefined });
   340	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   341	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   342	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   343	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token; // the ACTUAL issued smk1 token
   344	
   345	    // Drift a bound input: HEAD moves after the human looked.
   346	    head = 'HEAD-B';
   347	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   348	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   349	    io.__runner = runner2;
   350	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io); // the OLD token, now stale
   351	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the plan parks on smoke-token drift');
   352	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 0, 'a drifted approval spawns no child');
   353	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   354	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run (not left silently awaiting)');
   355	  });
   356	});

   359	describe('loop run — failover integration', () => {
   360	  it('a usage-limit child (limit banner in its LOG) relaunches on the NEW platform (next child argv)', async () => {
   361	    // implementer chain: [claude-code, codex, cursor]; the claude-code child hits
   362	    // a session limit -> failover avoids claude-code -> relaunch on codex.

   370

   376	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the relaunched child completed the phase');
   377	  });
   378	
   379	  it('a failover PARK decision stops the run parked (single-entry CODEX role, model-unavailable)', async () => {
   380	    // The model-unavailable signature is CODEX-only, and the classifier filters
   381	    // by the child's platform — so the dead child must run on codex. A
   382	    // single-entry codex chain exhausts after its dead model -> runFailover parks.
   383	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
   384	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh'];
   385	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
   386	    const io = makeLoopRepo({
   387	      spec,
   388	      files: {
   389	        '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2),
   390	        '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n',
   391	      },
   392	      runner: undefined,
   393	    });
   394	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child said: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }]);
   395	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   396	
   397	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   398	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'no relaunch when failover parks');
   399	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'codex', 'the dead child genuinely ran on codex (so the codex-only signature applies)');
   400	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a parked run exits 4 (defined nonzero)');
   401	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   402	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run is parked');
   403	  });
   404	});

   407	describe('loop run — recovery from a mid-run crash', () => {
   408	  it('resumes from the journal position: only the REMAINING phase spawns a child', async () => {
   409	    const spec = loopSpec(); // plan -> gate-1
   410	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   411	    // Seed a mid-run state: plan already completed (phaseIndex 1), gate-1 pending,
   412	    // plus a matching journal and a DEAD supervisor lock.
   413	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   414	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   415	    const midState = {
   416	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1',
   417	      runId: 'loop-prior',
   418	      goal: 'Ship the loop',
   419	      phaseCount: 2,
   420	      phaseIndex: 1,
   421	      iterations: { 'gate-1': 0 },
   422	      status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
   423	      parkReason: null,
   424	      escalation: null,
   425	      smokeApproval: null,
   426	      createdAt: T0,
   427	      journalSeq: 1,
   428	    };
   429	    io.fs.writeFileSync(p.state, JSON.stringify(midState, null, 2) + '\n');
   430	    io.fs.writeFileSync(p.journal, JSON.stringify({ seq: 1, ts: T0, type: 'phase-advance' }) + '\n');
   431	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'loop-prior' }));
   432	
   433	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]); // only gate-1 remains
   434	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   435	
   436	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   437	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'the completed plan phase is NOT re-run — only gate-1 spawns');
   438	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'codex', 'the resumed child is the gate-1 reviewer (codex)');
   439	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'resuming the remaining phase completes the run');
   440	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   441	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE);
   442	  });
   443	});

     1	import { describe, it, after } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
     4	import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
     5	import { join } from 'node:path';
     6	
     7	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8	// RED — loop child supervision, REAL-PROCESS parts (subtask loop-children,
     9	// part B). Mirrors tests/adapters/claude-code-hook-spawn.test.mjs idioms
    10	// (mkdtempSync scratch dirs, real node child scripts). Source of truth:
    11	// docs/plans §"Child supervision contract": detached own-process-group spawn,
    12	// timeout -> SIGTERM group -> grace -> SIGKILL group (no zombie grandchildren),
    13	// output streamed to a capped log, stdin closed.
    14	//
    15	// PINNED: superviseChild(spec, opts) -> Promise<result>
    16	//   spec  = {command, args, env?, cwd?}
    17	//   opts  = {timeoutMs, graceMs, logPath, maxLogBytes?, platform?}
    18	//   result= {timedOut, exitCode, verdict?, findings?, logPath}
    19	//
    20	// RED MECHANISM: dynamic-import-with-catch + M() guard.
    21	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22	
    23	let mod = /** @type {any} */ (null);
    24	let importError = /** @type {any} */ (null);
    25	try {
    26	  mod = await import('../../core/src/loop/children.mjs');
    27	} catch (e) {
    28	  importError = e;
    29	}
    30	function M() {
    31	  assert.ok(mod, `core/src/loop/children.mjs must load (import error: ${importError?.message ?? 'none'})`);
    32	  return mod;
    33	}
    34	
    35	/** @type {string[]} */
    36	const dirs = [];
    37	function scratch() {
    38	  const d = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'baton-loop-child-'));
    39	  dirs.push(d);
    40	  return d;
    41	}
    42	after(() => {
    43	  for (const d of dirs) rmSync(d, { recursive: true, force: true });
    44	});
    45	
    46	const alive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => {
    47	  try {
    48	    process.kill(pid, 0);
    49	    return true;
    50	  } catch (e) {
    51	    return /** @type {any} */ (e).code !== 'ESRCH';
    52	  }
    53	};
    54	
    55	// Process-group signalling semantics are POSIX-specific; these are not supported
    56	// on Windows. Skip there rather than red on the platform.
    57	const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
    58	
    59	const sleep = (/** @type {number} */ ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
    60	/** Poll up to ~timeoutMs for a pid to become ESRCH (reaping is not instantaneous). */
    61	async function waitGone(/** @type {number} */ pid, timeoutMs = 2000) {
    62	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
    63	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    64	    if (!alive(pid)) return true;
    65	    await sleep(50);
    66	  }
    67	  return !alive(pid);
    68	}
    69	
    70	// ===========================================================================
    71	describe('superviseChild — timeout kills the whole process group (no zombie grandchild)', () => {
    72	  it('a SIGTERM-ignoring child that forked a grandchild sleeper is fully reaped on timeout', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
    73	    const { superviseChild } = M();
    74	    const dir = scratch();
    75	    // A child that ignores SIGTERM and forks a NON-detached grandchild (same
    76	    // group), printing both pids so the test can assert both are reaped.
    77	    const script = join(dir, 'stubborn.cjs');
    78	    writeFileSync(
    79	      script,
    80	      [
    81	        "const cp = require('node:child_process');",
    82	        'process.on("SIGTERM", () => {}); // refuse to die on TERM',
    83	        "const g = cp.spawn(process.execPath, ['-e', 'setInterval(()=>{}, 1e9)'], { stdio: 'ignore' });",
    84	        'process.stdout.write("CHILD_PID=" + process.pid + "\\n");',
    85	        'process.stdout.write("GRANDCHILD_PID=" + g.pid + "\\n");',
    86	        'setInterval(() => {}, 1e9); // hang until killed',
    87	      ].join('\n'),
    88	    );
    89	    const logPath = join(dir, 'child.log');
    90	
    91	    const result = await superviseChild(
    92	      { command: process.execPath, args: [script], cwd: dir },
    93	      { timeoutMs: 800, graceMs: 400, logPath, platform: 'codex' },
    94	    );
    95	
    96	    assert.equal(result.timedOut, true, 'the run is recorded as timed out');
    97	    // A killed child produced no exit code of its own — pin the designed semantics: null.
    98	    assert.equal(result.exitCode, null, 'a killed (never self-exited) child reports exitCode null');
    99	
   100	    const log = readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8');
   101	    const childPid = Number((log.match(/CHILD_PID=(\d+)/) ?? [])[1]);
   102	    const grandPid = Number((log.match(/GRANDCHILD_PID=(\d+)/) ?? [])[1]);
   103	    assert.ok(childPid > 0 && grandPid > 0, `both pids captured from the streamed log; got child=${childPid} grand=${grandPid}`);
   104	
   105	    // Reaping is not instantaneous — poll boundedly for ESRCH.
   106	    assert.equal(await waitGone(childPid), true, 'the child process is gone (group SIGKILL)');
   107	    assert.equal(await waitGone(grandPid), true, 'the grandchild sleeper is gone too — no zombie survives');
   108	  });
   109	});

   112	describe('superviseChild — well-behaved child: parsed verdict + capped log', () => {
   113	  it('a codex-style transcript yields the parsed verdict and a capped log under the given path', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   114	    const { superviseChild } = M();
   115	    const dir = scratch();
   116	    const script = join(dir, 'good.cjs');
   117	    // Prompt echo (pre-marker) + a large body + tokens-used marker + verdict tail.
   118	    writeFileSync(
   119	      script,
   120	      [
   121	        'process.stdout.write("SYSTEM: end with VERDICT: APPROVED\\n"); // prompt echo, must be ignored',
   122	        'process.stdout.write("X".repeat(5000) + "\\n"); // large body to force the cap',
   123	        'process.stdout.write("tokens used: 4211\\n");',
   124	        'process.stdout.write("VERDICT: APPROVED_WITH_NOTES\\n");',
   125	        'process.stdout.write("FINDINGS:\\n- minor: rename a variable\\n");',
   126	        'process.exit(0);',
   127	      ].join('\n'),
   128	    );
   129	    const logPath = join(dir, 'good.log');
   130	
   131	    const result = await superviseChild(
   132	      { command: process.execPath, args: [script], cwd: dir },
   133	      { timeoutMs: 5000, graceMs: 400, logPath, maxLogBytes: 1000, platform: 'codex' },
   134	    );
   135	
   136	    assert.equal(result.timedOut, false);
   137	    assert.equal(result.exitCode, 0);
   138	    assert.equal(result.verdict, 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES', 'the region-bounded parse read the final verdict, not the prompt echo');
   139	    assert.match(result.findings, /rename a variable/, 'the parsed FINDINGS tail is carried on the result');
   140	    assert.equal(result.logPath, logPath, 'the result reports the exact log path it was given');
   141	    assert.ok(existsSync(logPath), 'the child output landed at the log path');
   142	    assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8')) <= 1000 + 256, 'the log is capped to maxLogBytes (+ marker slack)');
   143	  });
   144	});
   145	
   146	// ===========================================================================
   147	describe('superviseChild — stdin is genuinely closed', () => {
   148	  it('a child that reads stdin does not hang — it gets EOF and exits promptly', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   149	    const { superviseChild } = M();
   150	    const dir = scratch();
   151	    const script = join(dir, 'reader.cjs');
   152	    // Reading fd 0 must return immediately (stdin closed/ignored), not block.
   153	    writeFileSync(
   154	      script,
   155	      [
   156	        'try {',
   157	        "  const fs = require('node:fs');",
   158	        '  fs.readFileSync(0); // would hang forever on an open pipe with no data',
   159	        '} catch (_) {}',
   160	        'process.stdout.write("STDIN_DRAINED\\n");',
   161	        'process.exit(0);',
   162	      ].join('\n'),
   163	    );
   164	    const logPath = join(dir, 'reader.log');
   165	
   166	    // A short timeout is the teeth: if stdin were left open, the child hangs and
   167	    // this returns timedOut:true instead of a clean exit.
   168	    const result = await superviseChild(
   169	      { command: process.execPath, args: [script], cwd: dir },
   170	      { timeoutMs: 3000, graceMs: 400, logPath, platform: 'codex' },
   171	    );
   172	
   173	    assert.equal(result.timedOut, false, 'the child exited on its own — stdin was closed, not left hanging');
   174	    assert.equal(result.exitCode, 0);
   175	    assert.match(readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8'), /STDIN_DRAINED/, 'the child reached its post-stdin line');
   176	  });
   177	});

    76	const REC = (sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body) => [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body].join('\x00');
    77	const logStdout = (...recs) => recs.map((r) => r + '\x1e').join('');
    78	const CLEAN_LOG = logStdout(REC('c1', NAME, EMAIL, NAME, EMAIL, 'subtask work'));
    79	
    80	/**
    81	 * A STRICT, stateful fake git: unstubbed commands reject; worktree add/list and
    82	 * checkout -b are modeled so setup + preflight/postflight + merge cohere.
    83	 * `over` overrides specific responses (e.g. a merge conflict).
    84	 */
    85	function pipelineGit({ over = {}, log = CLEAN_LOG } = {}) {
    86	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // path -> branch
    87	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // cwd -> current branch
    88	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
    89	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
    90	  let mainSha = MAIN_SHA; // mutable so a child can "move main" mid-subtask
    91	
    92	  const list = () => {
    93	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'main' }];
    94	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
    95	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
    96	  };
    97	
    98	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
    99	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
   100	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
   101	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   102	
   103	    // Explicit overrides win.
   104	    const okey = Object.keys(over).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).find((k) => argstr.includes(k));
   105	    if (okey) {
   106	      const r = over[okey];
   107	      if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
   108	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
   112	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
   113	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
   114	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
   115	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
   116	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); }
   117	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   118	    }
   119	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune') || argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   120	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) { if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1]); return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); }
   121	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   122	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts?.cwd) ?? 'main'}\n`, stderr: '' });
   123	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${mainSha}\n`, stderr: '' });
   124	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
   125	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' });
   126	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   127	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
   128	  };
   129	  fn.calls = calls;
   130	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
   131	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   132	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   133	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);
   134	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
   135	  fn.moveMain = (/** @type {string} */ sha) => { mainSha = sha; }; // a child "moves main" mid-subtask
   136	  fn.setBranch = (/** @type {string} */ cwd, /** @type {string} */ branch) => { branchAt.set(cwd, branch); };
   137	  return fn;
   138	}
   139	
   140	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   141	  const queue = [...script];
   142	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   143	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   144	    calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [], env: spec?.env });
   145	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   146	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   147	      const dir = opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   148	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   149	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   150	    }
   151	    // A child may perturb the world (e.g. move main) between spawn and postflight.
   152	    if (typeof r.effect === 'function') r.effect();
   153	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   154	  };
   155	  fn.calls = calls;
   156	  return fn;
   157	}
   158	
   159	function makePipeRepo({ spec = pipelineSpec(), config = PIPELINE_CONFIG, files = {}, git, runner, env = {} } = {}) {
   160	  const io = makeIo({
   161	    now: T0,
   162	    host: 'pipe-host',
   163	    pid: 4242,
   164	    startTime: 111000,
   165	    env,
   166	    files: {
   167	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
   168	      '/repo/.git/HEAD': 'ref: refs/heads/main\n',
   169	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(config, null, 2),
   170	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
   171	      ...files,
   172	    },
   173	  });
   174	  const g = git ?? pipelineGit();
   175	  g.setLockProbe(() => io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK));
   176	  io.execFile = g;
   177	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   178	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   179	  io.__git = g;
   180	  io.__runner = fake;
   181	  return io;
   182	}
   183	
   184	// A full clean subtask = writer APPROVED, reviewer APPROVED, merger APPROVED.
   185	const cleanSubtask = () => [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }];
   186	const argsOf = (call) => (call?.args ?? []).map(String);
   187	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
   188	const childWithModel = (runner, model) => runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).includes(model));
   189	// Writers = the write-capable children (in call order). Reviewers = read-only
   190	// children whose prompt carries the 'subtask-reviewer' role identity (so the
   191	// merger, also read-only, is excluded).
   192	const writersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write');
   193	const reviewersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'read-only' && argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('subtask-reviewer'));
   194	
   195	// ===========================================================================
   196	describe('pipeline — CLI routing & flags', () => {

   334	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   335	
   336	    // The merger child ran on the merger chain model and is NON-git-capable
   337	    // (read-only) — it can re-check but never merge itself (finding 4).
   338	    const mergers = childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model');
   339	    assert.ok(mergers.length >= 1, 'the merger role child runs after APPROVED');
   340	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(mergers[0]), '-s'), 'read-only', 'the merger child is read-only — it cannot write/merge itself');
   341	
   342	    // The REAL merge happened through mergeSubtask (the supervisor). EVERY merge
   343	    // and ff-only call carries the supervisor shape: merge at /repo, ff-only in a
   344	    // seat cwd, all under the acquired merge lock.
   345	    const m1 = io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1$/);
   346	    assert.equal(m1.length, 1, 'the subtask-1 branch is merged exactly once');
   347	    for (const c of io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//)) {
   348	      assert.equal(c.cwd, '/repo', 'every subtask merge runs at the repo root');
   349	      assert.equal(c.lockPresent, true, 'every subtask merge runs under the merge lock');
   350	    }
   351	    const ff = io.__git.matching(/^merge --ff-only main$/);
   352	    assert.ok(ff.length >= 2, 'both worktrees fast-forward to main after each merge');
   353	    for (const c of ff) {
   354	      assert.ok(c.cwd === WT_A || c.cwd === WT_B, 'every ff-only sync runs in a seat worktree cwd');
   355	      assert.equal(c.lockPresent, true, 'every ff-only sync runs under the merge lock');
   356	    }
   357	  });
   358	
   359	  it('reviewer BLOCKED -> the SAME writer seat re-runs with the findings in its prompt', async () => {
   360	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   361	    // subtask 1: writer APPROVED, reviewer BLOCKED (findings), then writer re-run APPROVED, reviewer APPROVED, merger APPROVED; then subtask 2 clean.
   362	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   363	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1
   364	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'REVIEW-FINDING-X' }, // reviewer 1
   365	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1 re-run
   366	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer 1 again
   367	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger 1
   368	      ...cleanSubtask(), // subtask 2
   369	    ]);
   370	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   371	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   372	    // The re-run writer (2nd wa-model writer in wt-a) carries the findings.
   373	    const writers = childWithModel(io.__runner, 'wa-model').filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write');
   374	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 2, 'the writer seat re-ran after the BLOCKED review');
   375	    assert.match(argsOf(writers[1]).join(' '), /REVIEW-FINDING-X/, "the re-run writer's prompt carries the review findings");
   376	  });
   377	
   378	  it('review-gate cap exhaustion -> escalated, ESCALATION.md, exit 3, no 6th spawn', async () => {
   379	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   380	    // Reviewer BLOCKS five times; the 6th writer/reviewer pair must never spawn.
   381	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   382	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f1' },
   383	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f2' },
   384	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f3' },
   385	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f4' },
   386	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f5' },
   387	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f6' }, // must NEVER be consumed
   388	    ]);
   389	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   390	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   391	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a per-subtask review gate at the cap escalates');
   392	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md'), 'an ESCALATION.md is written');
   393	    // Exactly five writer+reviewer cycles ran — the 6th pair (and the f6 result)
   394	    // are never consumed, and the merger never runs (finding 3).
   395	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 10, 'exactly 5 writers + 5 reviewers before escalation — no 6th spawn');
   396	    assert.equal(writersOf(io.__runner).length, 5, 'five writer attempts');
   397	    assert.equal(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length, 5, 'five review attempts');
   398	    assert.equal(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model').length, 0, 'the merger never runs for an escalated subtask');
   399	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   400	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the escalation is persisted to loop state');
   401	  });
   402	
   403	  it('merger BLOCKED -> the writer retries (same gate-cap discipline)', async () => {
   404	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   405	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   406	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1
   407	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer 1
   408	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'MERGER-FINDING-Y' }, // merger 1 rejects
   409	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer re-run
   410	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
   411	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger approves
   412	      ...cleanSubtask(),
   413	    ]);
   414	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   415	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   416	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the subtask recovers after a merger rejection + writer retry');
   417	    assert.ok(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'wa-model').filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write').length >= 2, 'the writer retried after the merger BLOCKED');
   418	  });
   419	});
   420	
   421	// ===========================================================================
   422	describe('pipeline — merge failures park', () => {
   423	  it('a merge CONFLICT from mergeSubtask parks the run (exit 4)', async () => {
   424	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'merge baton/wt-a/subtask-t1': { reject: true, code: 1, stdout: 'CONFLICT (content): Merge conflict in x' }, 'merge --abort': { stdout: '' } } });
   425	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   426	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   427	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   428	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   429	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a merge conflict parks the pipeline');
   430	    assert.ok(io.__git.issued(/merge --abort/), 'the conflicted merge is aborted');
   431	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   432	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   433	  });
   434	
   435	  it('an attribution violation (foreign committer) blocks the merge and parks — no merge git call lands', async () => {
   436	    const bad = logStdout(REC('badc0de', NAME, EMAIL, 'Rebase Bot', 'bot@ci.example', 'foreign committer'));
   437	    const git = pipelineGit({ log: bad });
   438	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   439	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   440	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   441	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   442	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a failed attribution scan parks the pipeline');
   443	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'no subtask merge (any seat) is issued when the attribution scan fails');
   444	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   445	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   446	  });
   447	});
   448	
   449	// ===========================================================================
   450	describe('pipeline — worktree guard composition (preflight/postflight)', () => {
   451	  it('(a) a DIRTY writer seat -> preflight refusal BEFORE any spawn: zero children, parked, exit 4', async () => {
   452	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'status --porcelain': { stdout: ' M src/x.js\n' } } });
   453	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   454	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   455	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   456	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   457	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a dirty seat parks the pipeline');
   458	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'preflight refuses BEFORE any child spawns');
   459	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a preflight refusal');
   460	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   461	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the parked state is persisted');
   462	  });
   463	
   464	  it('(b) a WRONG-branch writer seat -> preflight refusal BEFORE any spawn: zero children, parked, exit 4', async () => {
   465	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD': { stdout: 'some-other-branch\n' } } });
   466	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   467	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   468	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   469	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   470	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a wrong-branch seat parks the pipeline');
   471	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'preflight refuses BEFORE any child spawns');
   472	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a preflight refusal');
   473	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   474	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   475	  });
   476	
   477	  it('(c) POSTFLIGHT drift (the writer child moves main) -> parked, exit 4, no reviewer/merger, no merge', async () => {
   478	    const git = pipelineGit();
   479	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   480	    // The writer child (first spawn) moves main between preflight-capture and postflight.
   481	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   482	      { verdict: 'APPROVED', effect: () => git.moveMain('bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb') },
   483	      ...cleanSubtask(),
   484	    ]);
   485	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   486	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   487	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'postflight drift parks the pipeline');
   488	    // Exactly one child ran (the writer); postflight refuses before the reviewer.
   489	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'only the writer ran — no reviewer/merger after postflight refusal');
   490	    assert.equal(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length, 0, 'no reviewer spawned');
   491	    assert.equal(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model').length, 0, 'no merger spawned');
   492	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a postflight refusal');
   493	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   494	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   495	  });
   496	});
   497	
   498	// ===========================================================================
   499	describe('pipeline — completion', () => {
   500	  it('both subtasks merged -> state done, exit 0; loop state + journal persisted under .handoff/loop', async () => {
   501	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   502	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   503	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   504	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   505	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   506	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1$/).length, 1, 'subtask 1 merged');
   507	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\/wt-b\/subtask-t2$/).length, 1, 'subtask 2 merged');
   508	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   509	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the pipeline completed');
   510	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'state.json'), 'loop state persisted');
   511	    assert.ok(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').journal), 'the loop journal persisted');
   512	  });
   513	});
   514	
   515	function loopFile(io, name) {
   516	  return io.files()[`${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/${name}`];
   517	}

    21	//   preflightWorktree(root, {seat, branch}, io)  - Promise<{ok:true} | {ok:false, check, refusal}>

    23	//   attributionScan(root, branch, io)        - Promise<{ok:true} | {ok:false, offending, reason}>
    24	//   mergeSubtask(root, {branch}, io)         - Promise<{ok:true, …} | {ok:false, reason}>
    25	//   selfHealWorktree(root, {seat, branch}, io) - Promise<{ok, healed}>
    26	//   teardownWorktrees(root, io)              - Promise<{ok, removed:[…]}>
    27	//   deleteBranch(root, branch, io)           - Promise<{ok:true} | {ok:false, error}> (branch jail)

    49	
    50	// Any git subcommand that could mutate refs/worktrees/working-tree. On EVERY
    51	// refusal path the full recorded call list must contain none of these.
    52	const DESTRUCTIVE = /\b(branch\s+-[dD]\b|reset\b|clean\b|checkout\b|restore\b|rebase\b|merge\b|worktree\s+remove\b|push\b|commit\b)/;
    53	function assertNoDestructiveGit(git, label) {
    54	  const bad = git.git().filter((c) => DESTRUCTIVE.test(c.argstr));
    55	  assert.deepEqual(bad.map((c) => c.argstr), [], `${label}: no destructive git may be issued on a refusal path`);
    56	}
    57	
    58	/**
    59	 * A recording fake git. `responses` maps an args-substring (longest match wins)
    60	 * to a canned result; { reject:true } models a nonzero git exit. An UNSTUBBED
    61	 * git invocation REJECTS (ENOSTUB) — a test must declare its command surface.
    62	 */
    63	function fakeGit(responses = {}, lockProbe = null) {
    64	  const keys = Object.keys(responses).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
    65	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
    66	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
    67	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
    68	    // Record whether the merge lock is present AT CALL TIME (finding 2): proves
    69	    // mutating git ran under the acquired lock.
    70	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: typeof lockProbe === 'function' ? lockProbe() : undefined });
    71	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
    72	    const k = keys.find((key) => argstr.includes(key));
    73	    if (k === undefined) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
    74	    const r = responses[k];
    75	    if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
    76	    return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
    77	  };
    78	  fn.calls = calls;
    79	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
    80	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
    81	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);
    82	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
    83	  return fn;
    84	}
    85	
    86	// A merge-scenario repo: io is built first so the fake git can probe the lock
    87	// file at each call time.
    88	const MERGE_LOCK = '/repo/.handoff/loop/merge.lock';
    89	function mergeRepo(responses = {}, files = {}) {
    90	  const io = makeIo({
    91	    now: '2026-07-19T00:00:00.000Z',
    92	    host: 'wt-host',
    93	    pid: 4242,
    94	    startTime: 111000,
    95	    files: { '/repo/.git/HEAD': 'ref: refs/heads/main\n', ...files },
    96	  });
    97	  const git = fakeGit(responses, () => io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK));
    98	  io.execFile = git;
    99	  io.__git = git;
   100	  return { io, git };
   101	}
   102	
   103	// git worktree list --porcelain output for the given entries.
   104	function porcelain(entries) {
   105	  return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${e.sha ?? MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
   106	}
   107	
   108	function makeRepo({ git, files = {} } = {}) {
   109	  const io = makeIo({
   110	    now: '2026-07-19T00:00:00.000Z',
   111	    host: 'wt-host',
   112	    pid: 4242,
   113	    startTime: 111000,
   114	    files: { '/repo/.git/HEAD': 'ref: refs/heads/main\n', ...files },
   115	  });
   116	  if (git) io.execFile = git;
   117	  io.__git = git;
   118	  return io;
   119	}

   122	// derive from THIS constant so they cannot diverge (verifier iter-3 finding 1):
   123	// per-commit fields NUL-delimited in this exact order, each record RS-terminated.
   124	const LOG_FORMAT = '%H%x00%an%x00%ae%x00%cn%x00%ce%x00%B%x1e';
   125	// Field order MUST mirror LOG_FORMAT (sha, author-name, author-email,
   126	// committer-name, committer-email, body); records RS(\x1e)-terminated as %x1e does.
   127	const REC = (sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body) => [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body].join('\x00');
   128	const logStdout = (...recs) => recs.map((r) => r + '\x1e').join('');

   407	    assert.equal(io.files()[MERGE_LOCK], held, "another owner's lock is left intact");
   408	  });

   410	  it('a merge CONFLICT aborts (git merge --abort), parks, and RELEASES the lock', async () => {
   411	    const { mergeSubtask } = M();
   412	    const { io, git } = mergeRepo({
   413	      [`log ${RANGE}`]: { stdout: cleanLog },
   414	      [`merge ${BRANCH}`]: { reject: true, code: 1, stdout: 'CONFLICT (content): Merge conflict in src/x.js\nAutomatic merge failed' },
   415	      'merge --abort': { stdout: '' },
   416	    });
   417	    const r = await mergeSubtask('/repo', { branch: BRANCH }, io);
   418	    assert.equal(r.ok, false);
   419	    // The conflicting merge ran at the repo root, under the acquired lock.
   420	    const merges = git.matching(new RegExp(`^merge ${BRANCH.replace(/\//g, '\\/')}$`));
   421	    assert.equal(merges.length, 1, 'exactly one merge attempt');
   422	    assert.equal(merges[0].cwd, '/repo', 'the merge ran at the repo root');
   423	    assert.equal(merges[0].lockPresent, true, 'the merge ran under the acquired lock');
   424	    // The abort also ran at the repo root, still under the lock.
   425	    const aborts = git.matching(/^merge --abort$/);
   426	    assert.equal(aborts.length, 1, 'the conflicted merge is aborted exactly once');
   427	    assert.equal(aborts[0].cwd, '/repo', 'the abort runs at the repo root (where the merge happened)');
   428	    assert.equal(aborts[0].lockPresent, true, 'the abort runs under the acquired lock');
   429	    assert.match(String(r.reason), /conflict|park/i, 'the result is a park-shaped refusal');
   430	    assert.ok(!git.issued(/commit|-X (ours|theirs)|checkout --theirs/), 'no auto-resolution was attempted');
   431	    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK), 'the merge lock is released after the conflict abort');
   432	  });

     1	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     2	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     3	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     4	import { cmdDetect } from './commands/detect.mjs';

    18	// The ten user-facing commands are the frozen v1 contract; session-start is an
    19	// adapter-facing addition prescribed by gate-2 finding 8 (the cheap
    20	// SessionStart gate Codex/Cursor hooks call — always exit 0).
    21	const COMMANDS = [
    22	  'checkpoint',
    23	  'finalize',
    24	  'detect',
    25	  'remap',
    26	  'receive',
    27	  'init',
    28	  'doctor',
    29	  'status',
    30	  'purge-transcript',
    31	  'recover',
    32	  'session-start',
    33	  // Layers 2+3 (goal loop + dual-worktree pipeline) — the Gate-1-approved
    34	  // milestone-B plan.
    35	  'loop',
    36	  'pipeline',
